- **smell-critic**: Adversarial read-only critic. Given an artifact plus smelltest's deterministic findings, it tries to REFUTE the agent's done/verified/cited claims. It can only accuse, never accept, edit, or run commands. Invoke before claiming completion on consequential work. It is invoked as a subagent (via the self-critique skill or by hand). The load-bearing blocking gate stays deterministic and model-free by design — a model is never the thing that blocks you. ([goolars/smelltest](https://github.com/goolars/smelltest/blob/HEAD/agents/smell-critic.md) · ★4)
- **smell-detector**: Read-only code-smell auditor invoked by the smell-scan skill's Workflow, one instance per smell category, all running in parallel. Reads the code, detects only the smells of its assigned category, and reports each finding with file, line range, evidence, a 0-100 confidence score, and the mapped refactoring.guru techniques. - **source-access**: The new feed pilot is executable with invented events. It demonstrates bounded state, explicit source clocks, gap quarantine and snapshot recovery. It does not yet connect a licensed production feed, grant trade authority, or establish customer adoption. These contracts apply to this new pilot; they do not replace the existing products' independently maintained source policies. ([beepboop2025/financial-evidence-skills](https://github.com/beepboop2025/financial-evidence-skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/source-access.md))
- **source-adapter-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for Harpe source adapters. Use after adding or changing a fetcher in packages/sources (pairs with the add-art-source skill), or to audit existing adapters for ArtItem-contract conformance and safe failure handling. Returns a findings report; it does NOT edit code. ([NullSense/harpe-site](https://github.com/NullSense/harpe-site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/source-adapter-reviewer.md))
